## 4. Architectural Summary & Lessons Learned

1. **Polysemous Overlap Safety**: Words like `bridge`, `squat`, `dog`, `port`, `crane`, `lead`, and `head` have legitimate dual meanings. The system correctly shields technical maritime inquiries while offering polite cross-domain confirmations for tricky off-topic questions.
2. **Typo Resilience**: Levenshtein distance matching against the in-memory database allows seafarers with typographical mistakes to seamlessly receive accurate technical answers without confronting insulting error messages.
3. **Fast-Path Acceleration**: Unambiguous maritime queries bypass LLM query rewrites and LLM intent classifiers, reducing end-to-end response latency from ~3.5s to ~1.2s.
4. **Zero-Hallucination Perimeter Guard**: True off-topic inquiries (recipes, entertainment, gaming, software programming) are intercepted before vector database retrieval, conserving computational tokens and maintaining strict domain integrity.
